S﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿ix strings. Twenty-one frets. One body that resonates.
T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿he low E hums. The high E answers.
